([cernoh/dendritic](https://github.com/cernoh/dendritic/tree/HEAD/modules/features/omp/home/agent/managed-skills/dendritic-host-package-addition))
- **dendritic-icl-snn**: DendriCL methodology for dendritic in-context learning in single-layer spiking neural networks. Demonstrates that a single dendritic compartment with online-LMS dynamics is sufficient for general-purpose ICL without attention, depth, or inference-time plasticity. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/spiking-neuromorphic/dendritic-icl-snn) · ★4)
- **dendritic-icl-snn**: Dendritic In-Context Learning (DendriCL) — single-layer compartmental SNN that achieves ICL via dendritic subthreshold dynamics implementing online LMS. Use when: designing SNN architectures for in-context learning, exploring biologically-plausible ICL mechanisms, studying dendritic computation, implementing seed-stable ICL beyond moderate task dimensions, analyzing compartmental neuron models. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/dendritic-icl-snn) · ★4)
- **dendritic-in-context-learning-snn**: DendriCL methodology for in-context learning in single-layer spiking neural networks using dendritic compartment dynamics. Use when: implementing ICL in biologically-plausible SNNs, designing compartmental spiking architectures, studying online LMS in dendrites, or building seed-stable ICL at super-dimensional task complexity. arXiv: 2607.02289 ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/spiking-neuromorphic/dendritic-in-context-learning-snn/dendritic-in-context-learning-snn) · ★4)
- **dendritic-in-context-learning-snn**: DendriCL methodology for dendritic in-context learning in single-layer spiking neural networks. Demonstrates that a single dendritic compartment with online-LMS dynamics implements complete in-context learning, eliminating the need for attention, depth, or inference-time plasticity. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/dendritic-in-context-learning-snn) · ★4)
- **dendritic-in-context-learning-snn**: DendriCL methodology for dendritic in-context learning in single-layer spiking neural networks. Shows that ICL requires neither attention, depth, nor inference-time plasticity: a single compartment with online-LMS dynamics is sufficient. Use when building SNNs with in-context learning capabilities, dendritic computation models, or biologically plausible learning mechanisms. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/dendritic-in-context-learning-snn) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **dendritic-nix**: Applies the Dendritic Pattern for structuring Nix flake configurations with flake-parts. Every file is a flake-parts module organized by feature (aspect), not by configuration class. Use when structuring Nix flakes, creating flake-parts modules, composing multi-host configs, organizing NixOS/home-manager/nix-darwin configuration, or sharing values across configuration classes. Triggers: nix flake structure, flake-parts modules, multi-host configs, aspect modules. Do NOT use for general Nix language questions, nixpkgs packaging, or NixOS module authoring unrelated to flake structure. ([geggo98/dotfiles](https://github.com/geggo98/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/modules/ai/_files/skills/dendritic-nix) · ★1)

- **dendrogram-visualization**: Use when you have a symmetric pairwise distance matrix (e.g., scDist distances between cell types) and need to cluster it hierarchically and render a dendrogram to check whether distances recapitulate known biological relationships such as immune cell lineages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/dendrogram-visualization))
- **dendrogram-visualization-of-lipidome-clustering**: Use when multiple lipidomes have been loaded and structurally embedded in a shared lipid space model, and you need to see which lipidomes cluster by structure/abundance or select a branch of similar lipidomes for reanalysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/dendrogram-visualization-of-lipidome-clustering))
